0103 — barbell standing ab rollerout

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0103 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A standing roll-out with the barbell. Requires strong abs and a firm back.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand upright with your feet shoulder-width apart and hold the barbell with both hands in front of your thighs.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0103.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Stand upright with your feet shoulder-width apart and hold the barbell with both hands in front of your thighs.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0103.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

Stand upright with your feet shoulder-width apart and hold the barbell with both hands in front of your thighs.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0103.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Straight Olympic barbell. For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0103.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand upright with your feet shoulder-width apart and hold the barbell with both hands in front of your thighs. 0103.json#/instructions/0
  2. Engage your core and slowly roll the barbell down towards the ground, keeping your back straight and your arms extended. 0103.json#/instructions/1
  3. Continue rolling the barbell forward until your body is fully extended and your hands are directly above your head. 0103.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pause for a moment, then slowly roll the barbell back towards your thighs, maintaining control and keeping your core engaged. 0103.json#/instructions/3
  5. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0103.json#/instructions/4

0104 — barbell standing back wrist curl

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0104 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A wrist curl with the bar held behind the back, building the grip and forearms.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand up straight with your feet shoulder-width apart and hold a barbell with an overhand grip. Rest the barbell on the back of your hands with your palms facing down and your fingers pointing towards your body.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0104.json#/instructions/0, 0104.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Stand up straight with your feet shoulder-width apart and hold a barbell with an overhand grip. Rest the barbell on the back of your hands with your palms facing down and your fingers pointing towards your body.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0104.json#/instructions/0, 0104.json#/instructions/1

Stand up straight with your feet shoulder-width apart and hold a barbell with an overhand grip.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0104.json#/instructions/0

For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0104.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand up straight with your feet shoulder-width apart and hold a barbell with an overhand grip. 0104.json#/instructions/0
  2. Rest the barbell on the back of your hands with your palms facing down and your fingers pointing towards your body. 0104.json#/instructions/1
  3. Keeping your upper arms stationary, exhale and curl your wrists upwards as far as possible. 0104.json#/instructions/2
  4. Hold the contracted position for a brief pause, then inhale and slowly lower the barbell back to the starting position. 0104.json#/instructions/3
  5. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0104.json#/instructions/4

0106 — barbell standing close grip curl

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0106 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A standing curl with a narrow grip. Builds the biceps.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand up straight with your feet shoulder-width apart and hold a barbell with an underhand grip, hands close together.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0106.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Stand up straight with your feet shoulder-width apart and hold a barbell with an underhand grip, hands close together.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0106.json#/instructions/0

For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0106.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand up straight with your feet shoulder-width apart and hold a barbell with an underhand grip, hands close together. 0106.json#/instructions/0
  2. Keep your elbows close to your torso and your upper arms stationary throughout the movement. 0106.json#/instructions/1
  3. Exhale as you curl the weights while contracting your biceps. Continue to raise the bar until your biceps are fully contracted and the bar is at shoulder level. 0106.json#/instructions/2
  4. Hold the contracted position for a brief pause as you squeeze your biceps. 0106.json#/instructions/3
  5. Inhale as you slowly begin to bring the barbell back to the starting position. 0106.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0106.json#/instructions/5

0107 — barbell standing front raise over head

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0107 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A front raise where the bar travels past shoulder height up to overhead. Targets the front delts.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a barbell in front of your thighs with an overhand grip.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0107.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a barbell in front of your thighs with an overhand grip. 0107.json#/instructions/0
  2. Keep your back straight and engage your core. 0107.json#/instructions/1
  3. Slowly raise the barbell in front of you, keeping your arms straight and your palms facing down. 0107.json#/instructions/2
  4. Continue lifting until the barbell is slightly above shoulder level. 0107.json#/instructions/3
  5. Pause for a moment at the top, then slowly lower the barbell back down to the starting position. 0107.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0107.json#/instructions/5

0108 — barbell standing leg calf raise

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0108 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A standing calf raise with the barbell on the shoulders. Builds the calves.

Proposed staging

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and place a barbell across your upper back.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand facing the cable machine with your feet shoulder-width apart. 0174.json#/instructions/0
  2. Hold the cable handle with both hands at chest level, palms facing down. 0174.json#/instructions/1
  3. Engage your core and rotate your torso to the right, pulling the cable across your body. 0174.json#/instructions/2
  4. As you rotate, pivot your back foot and allow your hips to rotate naturally. 0174.json#/instructions/3

  7. Repeat the movement on the opposite side. 0174.json#/instructions/6
  8. Continue alternating sides for the desired number of repetitions. 0174.json#/instructions/7

A seated row from a high pulley with an underhand grip. Pull to the torso and squeeze the back, keeping the back straight.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Sit on the seat facing the cable machine with your feet flat on the floor. 0208.json#/instructions/0
  2. Grasp the cable attachment with an underhand grip, palms facing up, and your hands shoulder-width apart. 0208.json#/instructions/1
  3. Keep your back straight and lean slightly forward from your hips. 0208.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pull the cable towards your torso by retracting your shoulder blades and squeezing your back muscles. 0208.json#/instructions/3
  5. Pause for a moment at the peak of the contraction, then slowly release the cable back to the starting position. 0208.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0208.json#/instructions/5
